- **vericontext**: Guides AI agents to follow VeriContext citation/claim rules when writing or editing documents (README, AGENTS.md, plans, etc.), and to verify with verify workspace that every claim is still current. When installed as a plugin, hooks enforce (1) a reminder while writing documents, and (2) automatic batch verification at the end of each turn. When only the skill is installed standalone, neither hook exists — the agent must follow this document's rules on its own, and can only add the optional git hook (commit/push blocking) on top. NOT for: modifying code itself, writing tests, build work. ([amsminn/vericontext](https://github.com/amsminn/vericontext/tree/HEAD/plugins/vericontext/skills/vericontext) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **verif**: Verify implementation by running lint, format check, type check, and post-implementation review ([lsagetlethias/fine-grained-proxy](https://github.com/lsagetlethias/fine-grained-proxy/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/verif) · ★12)
- **verif**: Vérification post-implémentation (format + lint + typecheck + tests) ([incubateur-ademe/mutafriches](https://github.com/incubateur-ademe/mutafriches/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/verif) · ★1)
- **verif**: Vérification post-implémentation (lint, typecheck, tests, build) ([MTES-MCT/fonds-prevention-argile](https://github.com/MTES-MCT/fonds-prevention-argile/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/verif) · ★1)
- **verifiability-first**: Turn the task into something verifiable and declare the success criterion before seeing results. Use when the request is vague about "done" ("make it work", "improve this"), when writing tests, when fixing a bug (write the failing reproduction first), or when comparing two approaches. Critical before any measurement or benchmark, where deciding the bar after seeing the numbers guarantees a "success". ([songjiun10-collab/Hncs](https://github.com/songjiun10-collab/Hncs/tree/HEAD/senior-engineer-mindset/verifiability-first) · ★9)
- **verifiable-credentials**: Verify and sign RDF graphs with W3C Data Integrity (eddsa-rdfc-2022 — standard Ed25519 over RDFC-1.0 + SHA-256) and resolve did:key/did:web to a verification key, with the opt-in sparq-vc crate. Use when you trust the SIGNER's key but need tamper-evidence / non-repudiation over an RDF answer (audit trails, data marketplaces, journalistic provenance, agent-to-agent exchange) — the standards-interop, vetted-crypto complement to the in-circuit ZK estate (sparq-zk). Covers sign/verify (sign_graph/verify_graph), ProofConfig binding, DidKeyResolver, and the pluggable did:web DidDocumentFetcher. ([sparq-org/sparq](https://github.com/sparq-org/sparq/tree/HEAD/skills/verifiable-credentials) · ★12)
- **verifiable-credentials-zk**: Patterns for handling W3C Verifiable Credentials inside a zero-knowledge pipeline — signature scheme trade-offs (BBS+ vs SD-JWT-VC vs EdDSA), commitment schemes (Pedersen, Poseidon), encoding of credential graphs to circuit-friendly representations, domain-separation discipline, and the public-input contract between the credential layer and the Noir circuit. Use when choosing crypto primitives, drafting the assumptions section, or pinning the verifier interface. ([sparq-org/sparq](https://github.com/sparq-org/sparq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/verifiable-credentials-zk) · ★12)
- **verifiable-goal**: Convert any task to a check that fails before and passes after. "Add validation" → tests for invalid inputs. "Fix bug" → reproduction + assertion. Use before declaring work done, when planning multi-step work, or when the user says "make sure this works". ([PlugFox/vixen-rs](https://github.com/PlugFox/vixen-rs/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/verifiable-goal) · ★3)
- **verifiable-intent**: Credential chain skill for delegating user intent to agents via Verifiable Intent (VI) ([zerkerlabs/treeship](https://github.com/zerkerlabs/treeship/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/verifiable-intent) · ★12)
